{"forms":[{"form":"commutator lengths","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"In #92;langlea,b#124;b²#61;1#92;rangle, the commutator length of aba#123;-2#125;ba is two, since it equals (aba#123;-1#125;b#123;-1#125;)(b#123;-1#125;a#123;-1#125;ba) but isn't a commutator.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The number of multiplicands needed, at a minimum, to express a given group element as a product of commutators."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The commutator length of #92;langlea,b#124;b²#61;1#92;rangle is at least two, since there's an element of commutator length two in it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The supremum, over all elements of a given group's derived subgroup, of their commutator lengths."]}],"upstream_line":2155594,"upstream_offset":6812307678,"upstream_record_sha256":"ead98bf7a6ca8ec93a4364836361bae36d8b2cc088ba052e561b69ffc96734df","word":"commutator length"}
